  mask-composite:exclude!important;

  opacity:.82!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(41,211,255,.08)!important;

<script id="AUREL_R139_FULL_BORDER_LED_METRICS_JS">
(function(){
  function norm(v){
    return String(v||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
  }

  function validCard(el){
    if(!el || el===document.body) return false;
    var r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
    var t=norm(el.textContent);
    return r.width>=220 && r.width<=620 && r.height>=120 && r.height<=360 && t.length<520;
  }

  function nearestCard(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var preferred=el.closest(".metric,.card,.panel,.analysis-feature,.analysis-card");
    if(preferred && validCard(preferred)) return preferred;
    var cur=el;
    for(var i=0;i<8 && cur && cur!==document.body;i++,cur=cur.parentElement){
      if(validCard(cur)) return cur;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mark(label){
    document.querySelectorAll("body *").forEach(function(el){
      if(norm(el.textContent)!==label) return;
      var card=nearestCard(el);
      if(card) card.classList.add("aurel-r139-full-led");
    });
  }

  function apply(){
    document.querySelectorAll(".aurel-r139-full-led").forEach(function(el){
      el.classList.remove("aurel-r139-full-led");
    });
    mark("ROA - Hiệu quả tài sản");
    mark("ROE - Hiệu quả vốn");
    mark("CIR - Chi phí / Thu nhập");
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      apply();
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState==="loading"){
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",schedule,{once:true});
  }else{
    schedule();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById("root")||document.body;
  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  document.addEventListener("click",function(){setTimeout(apply,40)},true);
})();
</script>
</body></html>

    return String(v||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
  }

  function validCard(el){

    return r.width>=220 && r.width<=620 && r.height>=120 && r.height<=360 && t.length<520;
  }

  function nearestCard(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var preferred=el.closest(".metric,.card,.panel,.analysis-feature,.analysis-card");
    if(preferred && validCard(preferred)) return preferred;
    var cur=el;
    for(var i=0;i<8 && cur && cur!==document.body;i++,cur=cur.parentElement){
      if(validCard(cur)) return cur;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mark(label){
    document.querySelectorAll("body *").forEach(function(el){
      if(norm(el.textContent)!==label) return;
      var card=nearestCard(el);
      if(card) card.classList.add("aurel-r139-full-led");
    });
  }

  function apply(){
    document.querySelectorAll(".aurel-r139-full-led").forEach(function(el){
      el.classList.remove("aurel-r139-full-led");
    });
    mark("ROA - Hiệu quả tài sản");
    mark("ROE - Hiệu quả vốn");
    mark("CIR - Chi phí / Thu nhập");
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      apply();
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState==="loading"){
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",schedule,{once:true});
  }else{
    schedule();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById("root")||document.body;
  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  document.addEventListener("click",function(){setTimeout(apply,40)},true);
})();
</script>

<script id="AUREL_R139_FULL_BORDER_LED_METRICS_JS">
(function(){
  function norm(v){
    return String(v||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
  }

  function validCard(el){
    if(!el || el===document.body) return false;
    var r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
    var t=norm(el.textContent);
    return r.width>=220 && r.width<=620 && r.height>=120 && r.height<=360 && t.length<520;
  }

  function nearestCard(el){
    if(!el) return null;
    var preferred=el.closest(".metric,.card,.panel,.analysis-feature,.analysis-card");
    if(preferred && validCard(preferred)) return preferred;
    var cur=el;
    for(var i=0;i<8 && cur && cur!==document.body;i++,cur=cur.parentElement){
      if(validCard(cur)) return cur;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function mark(label){
    document.querySelectorAll("body *").forEach(function(el){
      if(norm(el.textContent)!==label) return;
      var card=nearestCard(el);
      if(card) card.classList.add("aurel-r139-full-led");
    });
  }

  function apply(){
    document.querySelectorAll(".aurel-r139-full-led").forEach(function(el){
      el.classList.remove("aurel-r139-full-led");
    });
    mark("ROA - Hiệu quả tài sản");
    mark("ROE - Hiệu quả vốn");
    mark("CIR - Chi phí / Thu nhập");
  }

  var queued=false;
  function schedule(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      apply();
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState==="loading"){
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",schedule,{once:true});
  }else{
    schedule();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById("root")||document.body;
  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  document.addEventListener("click",function(){setTimeout(apply,40)},true);
})();
</script>
</body></html>

=== Phân tích chỉ số #2 @ 1048505 ===
;
      inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
    });

    /* Một cặp requestAnimationFrame chung: tất cả bắt đầu đúng cùng frame. */
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        cleanup();
        instances.forEach(function(inst){
          inst.current.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.next.style.transition='transform '+MOVE+'ms '+EASE;
          inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(-100%,0,0)';
          inst.next.style.transform='translate3d(0,0,0)';
        });

        setTimeout(function(){
          cleanup();
          instances.forEach(function(inst){
            inst.current.style.transition='none';
            inst.current.style.transform='translate3d(100%,0,0)';
            var old=inst.current;
            inst.current=inst.next;
            inst.next=old;
            inst.index=ni%inst.list.length;
          });
          globalIndex=ni;
          animating=false;
          scheduleMaster();
        },MOVE+40);
      });
    });
  }

  function run(){
    mount(document.querySelector('.sidebar'),SIDE,'side');
    mount(document.querySelector('.ref-hero'),HERO,'hero');
    scheduleMaster();
  }

  preload(HERO.concat(SIDE));

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});
  }else{
    run();
  }

  var root=document.getElementById('root')||document.body;
  var queued=false;
  new MutationObserver(function(){
    if(queued) return;
    queued=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      queued=false;
      cleanup();
      run();
    });
  }).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

  function moneyOnly(rows){
    return rows.filter(function(r){
      var n=ascii(r.name);
      return Number.isFinite(Number(r.value)) && r.value!==0 &&
        r.unit!=='%' && !/(roe|roa|cir|ty le|ratio)/.test(n);
    }).slice(0,5);
  }
  function donutParts(rows){
    var vals=moneyOnly(rows).map(function(r){
      return {name:r.name,value:Math.abs(Number(r.value)),display:r.display};
    });
    var total=vals.reduce(function(s,r){return s+r.value},0);
    if(!total)return [];
    return vals.map(function(r,i){
      return {name:r.name,display:r.display,pct:r.value/total*100,color:colors[i%colors.length]};
    });
  }
  function grad(parts){
    if(!parts.length)return 'conic-gradient(#e6edf3 0 100%)';
    var a=0,segs=[];
    parts.forEach(function(p){
      var s=a;a+=p.pct;
      segs.push(p.color+' '+s.toFixed(2)+'% '+a.toFixed(2)+'%');
    });
    if(a<100)segs.push('#eef3f7 '+a.toFixed(2)+'% 100%');
    return 'conic-gradient('+segs.join(',')+')';
  }
  function panel(){
    var root=document.getElementById('root');
    if(!root)return null;
    return root.querySelector('.ref-key-panel');
  }
  function render(){
    var root=document.getElementById('root'),p=panel();
    var nav=document.querySelector('#nav button.active[data-page="overview"]');
    if(!root||!p||!nav)return;

    var year=norm((document.getElementById('year-select')||{}).value)||'—';
    var title=p.querySelector('.ref-panel-title h2,.section-top h2,h2');
    if(title)title.textContent='Báo cáo tài chính';
    var sm=p.querySelector('.ref-panel-head small,.section-top small');
    if(sm)sm.textContent='Giá trị năm '+year;

    var tabs=p.querySelector('.r59-report-tabs');
    if(!tabs){
      tabs=document.createElement('div');
      tabs.className='r59-report-tabs';
      var hd=p.querySelector('.ref-panel-head,.section-top');
      if(hd)hd.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',tabs); else p.insertBefore(tabs,p.firstChild);
    }
    tabs.innerHTML='';
    Object.keys(labels).forEach(function(id){
      var b=document.createElement('button');
      b.type='button';
      b.setAttribute('data-report-tab',id);
      b.setAttribute('onclick',"window.AURELReportSwitch('"+id+"');return false;");
      b.textContent=labels[id];
      if(id===active)b.className='active';
      tabs.appendChild(b);
    });

    var old=p.querySelector('.r59-report-body,.r61-report-body');
    if(old)old.remove();

    var all=sourceRows(root,p);
    var rows=rowsFor(all,active);
    var body=document.createElement('div');
    body.className='r61-report-body';

    var left=document.createElement('div');
    if(!rows.length){
      left.className='r61-empty';
